### 25 L25 2026-09-28T09:29:50.805Z message id=2270712f
TEXT # REQ-1 自检脚本

本目录提供 Issue #3（REQ-1 身份与访问）的可重复自检，覆盖注册、登录、找回密码、
登出与修改密码的界面可观测状态与后端行为。

## 运行方式

在仓库根目录：

```bash
cd e2e && npm install     # 只装 playwright-core（不下载浏览器）
cd .. && node e2e/req1.spec.mjs
```

脚本自己完成以下步骤：

1. `frontend` 执行 `npm run build`（`SKIP_BUILD=1` 且已有 `frontend/dist` 时跳过）；
2. 用临时 `DATA_DIR` 与端口 `3912`（可用 `PORT` 覆盖，不使用 3000）启动
   `backend/src/server.js`，由 backend 托管构建后的前端（与平台交付入口一致）；
3. 用 Playwright 驱动真实 Chromium 逐条断言需求（见 `req1.spec.mjs` 中的 `R1..R5` 分组）；
4. 打印 `commit`、`frontend/dist/index.html` 哈希、端口/数据目录/浏览器路径与 PASS 汇总；
5. 结束前停止自启服务并删除临时数据目录（`KEEP_DATA=1` 可保留）。

退出码 0 表示全部断言通过，非 0 表示存在失败项（失败项会单独列出）。

## 环境变量

| 变量 | 说明 |
| --- | --- |
| `PORT` | 服务端口，默认 3912（3000 保留给官方评测） |
| `CHROMIUM_PATH` | 浏览器可执行文件；默认探测环境内 chromium，再退回 playwright 自带 |
| `SKIP_BUILD=1` | 跳过前端构建，复用已有 `frontend/dist` |
| `KEEP_DATA=1` | 保留临时数据目录 |

## 后端契约级自检

API 与持久化状态另由 `backend/test/req1.test.mjs` 覆盖：

```bash
cd backend && npm test
```

// Idempotent seed data, provisioned on every normal start. If the database
// already contains users, seeding is skipped so user-created data survives
// restarts within the same run.
export function seed(db) {
  const count = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users').get().c;
  if (count > 0) return;

  const now = Date.now();
  const iso = (minutesAgo) => new Date(now - minutesAgo * 60_000).toISOString();

  const tx = db.transaction(() => {
    insertUsers(db, iso);
    const org = insertOrg(db, iso);
    const repos = insertRepos(db, iso);
    seedWebAppContent(db, repos.webApp, iso);
    seedPlaygroundContent(db, repos.playground, iso);
    seedSecretApiContent(db, repos.secretApi, iso);
    seedAcmeDocsContent(db, repos.acmeDocs, iso);
    seedSecretResearchContent(db, repos.secretResearch, iso);
    seedForkConflict(db, repos.acmeDocs, iso);
    insertRepoGrants(db, repos);
    seedIssuesAndPulls(db, repos.webApp, iso);
    seedReviewWorkflow(db, iso);
    insertActivity(db, repos, iso);
    const demoOrg = insertDemoOrg(db, iso);
    seedDemo

escription, visibility, defaultBranch, createdAt) {
  db.prepare(

     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`

  db.prepare('INSERT INTO branches (repo_id, name, head_commit_id) VALUES (?, ?, NULL)').run(repo.id, defaultBranch);
  return repo;
}

// REQ-3-2-2 fork-conflict seed: `acme-docs-fork` already exists in
// alice-dev's personal namespace, so creating a fork with that name from the
// fork form must be rejected without creating a repository.
function seedForkConflict(db, sourceRepo, iso) {
  const alice = user(db, 'alice-dev');
  db.transaction(() =>
    forkRepository(db, {
      sourceRepo,
      owner: { type: 'user', id: alice.id, name: alice.username },
      name: 'acme-docs-fork',
      visibility: 'private',
      authorId: alice.id,
      createdAt: iso(60 * 24 * 10),
    })

function nextNumber(db, repoId) {
  const row = db.prepare('SELECT next_number FROM repo_counters WHERE repo_id = ?').get(repoId);
  const n = row.next_number;
  db.prepare('UPDATE repo_counters SET next_number = ? WHERE repo_id = ?').run(n + 1, repoId);
  return n;
}

function headCommit(db, repo, branch) {
  return db.prepare('SELECT head_commit_id FROM branches WHERE repo_id = ? AND name = ?').get(repo.id, branch).head_commit_id;
}

function seedIssuesAndPulls(db, repo, iso) {
  const alice = user(db, 'alice-dev').id;
  const bob = user(db, 'bob-reviewer').id;
  const eve = user(db, 'eve-reader').id;

  db.prepare("INSERT INTO labels (repo_id, name, color) VALUES (?, 'bug', '#d73a4a')").run(repo.id);
  db.prepare("INSERT INTO labels (repo_id, name, color) VALUES (?, 'enhancement', '#a2eeef')").run(repo.id);
  const milestone = db
    .prepare("INSERT INTO milestones (repo_id, title, description, due_on) VALUES (?, 'v1.0', 'First milestone', NULL)")
    .run(repo.id);

  // Issue #1: Improve onboarding (open, one discussion comment).
  const issue1 = nextNumber(db, repo.id);
  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO issues (repo_id, number, title, body, author_id, status, created_at, updated_at)
     VALUES (?, ?, 'Improve onboarding', 'Describe the onboarding improvement.', ?, 'open', ?, ?)`
  ).run(repo.id, issue1, eve, iso(60 * 24 * 3), iso(60 * 24 * 3));
  const issue1Id = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM issues WHERE repo_id = ? AND number = ?').get(repo.id, issue1).id;
  db.prepare('INSERT INTO issue_comments (issue_id, author_id, body, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)').run(
    issue1Id,
    bob,
    'We should split onboarding into short, guided steps.',
    iso(60 * 24 * 3 - 30)
  );
  // The target issue starts with one applied label and one assignee; the
  // `enhancement` label and the `v1.0` milestone stay unapplied for the
  // metadata pickers.
  db.prepare(
    "INSERT INTO issue_labels (issue_id, label_id) SELECT ?, id FROM labels WHERE repo_id = ? AND name = 'bug'"
  ).run(issue1Id, repo.id);
  db.prepare('INSERT INTO issue_assignees (issue_id, user_id) VALUES (?, ?)').run(issue1Id, bob);
  db.prepare("INSERT INTO activity (repo_id, actor_id, type, data, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, 'opened_issue', ?, ?)").run(
    repo.id,
    eve,
    JSON.stringify({ issue: issue1, title: 'Improve onboarding' }),
    iso(60 * 24 * 3)
  );

  // Issue #2: Legacy welcome text (closed).
  const issue2 = nextNumber(db, repo.id);
  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO issues (repo_id, number, title, body, author_id, status, created_at, updated_at)
     VALUES (?, ?, 'Legacy welcome text', 'The legacy welcome text should be retired.', ?, 'closed', ?, ?)`
  ).run(repo.id, issue2, alice, iso(60 * 24 * 8), iso(60 * 24 * 7));

  // PR #1: Fix search (open) — feature-search → main, authored by the seeded
  // Write contributor alice-dev. The PR-list filtering scenario filters the

function insertActivity(db, repos, iso) {
  const events = [
    [repos.webApp.id, user(db, 'alice-dev').id, 'pushed', { branch: 'main', commit: 'Document search flow' }, iso(60 * 24 * 12)],
    [repos.webApp.id, user(db, 'alice-dev').id, 'opened_issue', { number: 2, title: 'Legacy welcome text' }, iso(60 * 24 * 8)],
  ];
  for (const [repoId, actorId, type, data, at] of events) {
    db.prepare('INSERT INTO activity (repo_id, actor_id, type, data, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)').run(
      repoId,
      actorId,
      type,
      JSON.stringify(data),
      at
    );
  }
}

test('REQ-5-1-1 list & filter', async () => {
  let r = await anonymous('GET', `${REPO}/issues`);
  assert.equal(r.status, 200);
  const all = r.data.issues;
  assert.ok(all.some((i) => i.title === 'Improve onboarding' && i.state === 'open'));
  assert.ok(all.some((i) => i.title === 'Legacy welcome text' && i.state === 'closed'));
  // rows expose number/title/author/labels/update time
  const row = all.find((i) => i.title === 'Improve onboarding');
  assert.ok(row.number > 0 && row.author && Array.isArray(row.labels) && row.updatedAt);
  assert.ok(row.labels.some((l) => l.name === 'bug'));

  r = await anonymous('GET', `${REPO}/issues?state=open&q=Improve`);
  assert.equal(r.data.issues.length, 1);
  assert.equal(r.data.issues[0].title, 'Improve onboarding');

  r = await anonymous('GET', `${REPO}/issues?state=closed&q=Legacy welcome text`);
  assert.equal(r.data.issues.length, 1);
  assert.equal(r.data.issues[0].title, 'Legacy welcome text');

  r = await anonymous('GET', `${REPO}/issues?state=closed&q=Improve onboarding`);
  assert.equal(r.data.issues.length, 0);

  r = await anonymous('GET', `${REPO}/issues?label=bug`);
  assert.ok(r.data.issues.every((i) => i.labels.some((l) => l.name === 'bug')));
});

function startServer() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['src/server.js'], {
      cwd: path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..'),
      env: process.env,
      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    });
    let out = '';
    child.stdout.on('data', (d) => {
      out += d.toString();
      const m = out.match(/listening on http:\/\/([^:]+):(\d+)/);
      if (m) resolve({ child, port: Number(m[2]) });
    });
    child.stderr.on('data', (d) => process.stderr.write(d));
    child.on('exit', (code) => reject(new Error(`server exited early: ${code}`)));
  });
}

const { child, port } = await startServer();

// REQ-2 organization governance acceptance tests: runs the real server on an
// ephemeral port with a temporary data directory and drives the API contract.
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';

process.env.DATA_DIR = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'collab-org-test-'));
process.env.HOST = '127.0.0.1';
process.env.PORT = '0';

const { spawn } = await import('node:child_process');

function startServer() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['src/server.js'], {
      cwd: path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..'),
      env: process.env,
      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    });
    let out = '';
    child.stdout.on('data', (d) => {
      out += d.toString();
      const m = out.match(/listening on http:\/\/([^:]+):(\d+)/);
      if (m) resolve({ child, port: Number(m[2]) });
    });
    child.stderr.on('data', (d) => process.stderr.write(d));
    child.on('exit', (code) => reject(new Error(`server exited early: ${code}`)));
  });
}

const { child, port } = await startServer();
const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;

// Independent cookie jars per account so multiple sessions can coexist.
const jars = {};
async function call(method, url, body, as = null) {
  const headers = {};
  if (body !== undefined) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
  if (as && jars[as]) headers['Cookie'] = jars[as];
  const res = await fetch(`${base}${url}`, {
    method,
    headers,
    body: body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
  });
  const setCookie = res.headers.get('set-cookie');
  if (setCookie && as) jars[as] = setCookie.split(';')[0];
  let data = null;
  try {
    data = await res.json();
  } catch {}
  return { status: res.status, data };
}

async function signIn(as, login, password = 'Valid-password-123!') {
  const r = await call('POST', '/api/auth/login', { login, password }, as);
  assert.equal(r.status, 200, `sign in as ${login} failed: ${JSON.stringify(r.data)}`);
  return r;
}

      const user = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ?').get(subjectName);

      subjectId = user.id;

      const team = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM teams WHERE name = ?').get(subjectName);
      if (!team) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Team not found', field: 'subjectName' });
      if (req.repo.owner_type === 'org' && team.org_id !== req.repo.owner_id) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Team must belong to the organization', field: 'subjectName' });
      }
      subjectId = team.id;
    }
    // Only one direct role grant per subject and repository: saving the same
    // or a different role replaces the stored record instead of duplicating.
    const existing = db
      .prepare('SELECT id FROM grants WHERE repo_id = ? AND subject_type = ? AND subject_id = ?')
      .get(req.repo.id, subjectType, subjectId);
    if (existing) {
      db.prepare('UPDATE grants SET role = ? WHERE id = ?').run(role, existing.id);
      return res.json({ grant: { id: existing.id, subjectType, subjectName, role } });
    }
    const info = db
      .prepare('INSERT INTO grants (repo_id, subject_type, subject_id, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)')
      .run(req.repo.id, subjectType, subjectId, role);
    return res.status(201).json({ grant: { id: info.lastInsertRowid, subjectType, subjectName, role } });
  });

    const { role } = req.body || {};

      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Role is invalid', field: 'role' });

      .prepare('SELECT * FROM grants WHERE id = ? AND repo_id = ?')
      .get(Number(req.params.grantId), req.repo.id);
    if (!grant) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Not found' });

requirements.yaml 全文以 **`acme-docs`**（291 处，`web-app` 出现 0 次）为中央种子仓库：REQ-3-1 搜索种子、REQ-2-2-3 团队授权种子、REQ-4 分支/提交/代码搜索种子、REQ-5 三个种子 issue、REQ-6 两个种子 PR 全部挂在它上面。此前实现把这些放在 `acme/web-app`（共享基础时期的内部命名，未见需求依据），`acme-docs` 被实现成一个只有 main 分支的独立文档仓——官方按需求场景验收会直接失败。已修复（bb9bbe6）：

1. `web-app` 改名 `acme-docs`（分支/提交/issue/PR/授权按 repo_id 保留）；删除旧的独立文档仓；fork 冲突种子改从 acme-docs 派生。

3. 新增 Open PR **`Improve onboarding`**（improve-onboarding→main，作者 bob-reviewer——保持 Fix search 是 alice 唯一 Open PR，满足 REQ-6-2-1 作者过滤；bob 保留为评审者）。
4. acme 组织补 `platform-team→frontend-team→frontend-child` 团队层级（REQ-2-2-3 Manage access 种子：组织 Acme Demo + 仓库 acme-docs + 成员 bob-reviewer + 团队 frontend-team）。

6. 同步更新 backend 测试与 e2e 脚本（eec19cf），acceptance 测试固化新种子事实。
